Private Wealth Management has been recognized in several categories across <a href="https://www.euromoney.com/awards/private-banking-awards/pb-2026-results/" target="_blank">Euromoney’s Private Banking Awards 2026</a>, announced on March 20, 2026. The awards celebrate the achievements across the private banking and wealth management industry within the review period (July 1, 2024 to June 30, 2025), with winners determined by a panel of private banking specialists. Investment advisory services through Goldman Sachs &amp; Co. LLC (“GS&amp;Co.”). Brokerage services offered by GS&amp;Co., member FINRA/SIPC. © 2026 The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc. All rights reserved. <br />
 *Rating announced 03.20.26. Review period Q3’24-Q2’25. Rating by Euromoney. <br />
 <br />
 <sup>2</sup> Goldman Sachs Private Wealth Management has been recognized as the Best Private Bank for Net Worth over $25M, Best Private Bank for Access to Private Equity, Best Private Bank (North America), and Best Private Bank for Sustainable Investing (North America) <a href="https://gfmag.com/award/winner-announcements/press-release-global-finance-names-the-worlds-best-private-banks-2026/" target="_blank">by Global Finance for their World’s Best Private Bank Awards 2026</a>, announced on October 23, 2025. The awards celebrate the achievements across the private banking and wealth management industry within the review period (April 1, 2024 to March 31, 2025), with winners determined by a panel of private banking specialists.</span></p>
